Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the intervals object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 974. The moduli space has dimension at most 97 with 4 connected components.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_974(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 974. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^97(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any intervals module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 698. The projective resolution has length at most 199 with Betti numbers bounded by 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 698 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the intervals structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 199. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
